3756	  private servoTo(tx: number, ty: number, spd: number, accel: number): void {
3757	    const d0 = Math.hypot(tx - this.cx, ty - this.cy) || 1;
3758	    const ex = ((tx - this.cx) / d0) * spd;
3759	    const ey = ((ty - this.cy) / d0) * spd;
3760	    if (this.vx < ex) {
3761	      this.vx += accel;
3762	      if (this.vx < 0 && ex > 0) this.vx += accel;
3763	    } else if (this.vx > ex) {
3764	      this.vx -= accel;
3765	      if (this.vx > 0 && ex < 0) this.vx -= accel;
3766	    }
3767	    if (this.vy < ey) {
3768	      this.vy += accel;
3769	      if (this.vy < 0 && ey > 0) this.vy += accel;
3770	    } else if (this.vy > ey) {
3771	      this.vy -= accel;
3772	      if (this.vy > 0 && ey < 0) this.vy -= accel;
3773	    }
3774	  }
3775	
3776	  /** EoC 旋转追踪：visAngle 向 target 以 rate 步进（最短弧，cs:19975-20000） */
3777	  private applyEocRotation(target: number, rate: number): void {
3778	    let t = target;
3779	    while (t < 0) t += Math.PI * 2;
3780	    while (t > Math.PI * 2) t -= Math.PI * 2;
3781	    let a = this.visAngle;
3782	    while (a < 0) a += Math.PI * 2;
3783	    while (a > Math.PI * 2) a -= Math.PI * 2;
3784	    let diff = t - a;
3785	    if (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
3786	    if (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
3787	    this.visAngle = a + diff * Math.min(1, rate * 10);
3788	  }

252	let boundWorld: ScaleStatsWorld | null = null;
253	
254	export function bindScaleStatsWorld(ctx: ScaleStatsWorld | null): void { boundWorld = ctx; }
255	
256	export function scaleStatsWorld(): ScaleStatsWorld { return boundWorld ?? CLASSIC_SCALE_WORLD; }
257	
258	/** World → ScaleStatsWorld 投影（Game.afterWorldLoad 用；anyNpc 由实体桶实现） */
259	export function scaleStatsWorldOf(
260	  world: Pick<World, 'difficulty' | 'seedFlags' | 'flags'>,
261	  anyNpc: (type: number) => boolean,
262	): ScaleStatsWorld {
263	  return {
264	    difficulty: mainDifficulty(world),
265	    hardMode: !!world.flags?.hardMode,
266	    getGoodWorld: !!world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld,
267	    downedPlantBoss: !!world.flags?.['downed_262'],
268	    anyNpc,
269	    activePlayerCount: 1,   // 单机（NPC.GetActivePlayerCount :18518 netMode==0 → 1）
270	  };
271	}
272

274	// NPC.ScaleStats（NPC.cs:18081-18105）
275	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
276	export function scaleStats(
277	  s: ScalingStats,
278	  ctx: ScaleStatsWorld = scaleStatsWorld(),
279	  activePlayersCount?: number | null,
280	  strengthOverride?: number | null,
281	): void {
282	  // 入口门（:18083）：NeedsExpertScaling 白名单 或 通用门
283	  //   lifeMax>5 && damage!=0 && !friendly && !townNPC
284	  //   → **Boss 不豁免**（boss 不在排除条件里）；ProjectNPC（25/30/…）靠白名单进入
285	  if (NEEDS_EXPERT_SCALING.has(s.type) || (s.lifeMax > 5 && s.damage !== 0 && !s.friendly && !s.townNPC)) {
286	    s.difficulty = strengthOverride != null ? strengthOverride : ctx.difficulty;   // :18086
287	    if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT && ctx.hardMode) scaleStatsForExpertHardmode(s, ctx);  // :18087-18089
288	    scaleStatsByDifficulty(s, ctx);                                                 // :18090
289	    if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT) {
290	      scaleStatsByPlayerCount(s, activePlayersCount != null ? activePlayersCount : ctx.activePlayerCount);  // :18091-18094
291	    }
292	    // :18096-18100 弹体 NPC 以外 lifeMax 下限 6（经典档同样生效）
293	    if ((s.type < 0 || !PROJECTILE_NPC.has(s.type)) && s.lifeMax < 6) s.lifeMax = 6;
294	    // :18101-18103 defDamage/defDefense/defLifeMax 快照 → 调用侧另存 baseDamage/baseDefense
295	  }
296	}
297	
298	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
299	// NPC.ScaleStats_ByDifficulty（NPC.cs:18106-18116）
300	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
301	function scaleStatsByDifficulty(s: ScalingStats, ctx: ScaleStatsWorld): void {
302	  if (s.type < 0 || !PROJECTILE_NPC.has(s.type)) {                       // :18108-18112
303	    s.lifeMax = Math.trunc(f32(s.lifeMax * sampleCurve(ENEMY_MAX_LIFE_MULTIPLIER, s.difficulty)));
304	    s.value = Math.trunc(f32(s.value * sampleCurve(ENEMY_MONEY_DROP_MULTIPLIER, s.difficulty)));
305	  }
306	  s.damage = getAttackDamageScaledByDifficulty(s.damage, s.difficulty);  // :18113
307	  s.knockBackResist = getKnockbackMultiplierScaledByDifficulty(s.knockBackResist, s.difficulty);  // :18114
308	  scaleStatsByDifficultyTweaks(s, ctx);                                  // :18115
309	}
310	
311	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
312	// NPC.ScaleStats_ByDifficulty_Tweaks（NPC.cs:18118-18447）
313	//   num=伤害系数（Classic→Expert）/ num2=生命 Classic→Expert / num3=生命 Expert→Master
314	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
315	function scaleStatsByDifficultyTweaks(s: ScalingStats, ctx: ScaleStatsWorld): void {
316	  let num = 1.0;   // damage
317	  let num2 = 1.0;  // lifeMax (classic→expert)
318	  let num3 = 1.0;  // lifeMax (expert→master)
319	  const type = s.type;

321	  // ---- getGoodWorld 世吞/骷髅王/血肉墙本体降容联动（:18125-18152）----
322	  //   6 世吞头 且 AnyNPCs(13)（世吞在场）→ 大师生命数 ×0.85、伤害 ×1.5、专家防 +2

324	  //   24 火妖 且 AnyNPCs(113)（血肉墙在场）→ 同上、专家防 +10
325	  if (ctx.getGoodWorld) {
326	    if (type === 6 && ctx.anyNpc(13)) {
327	      num3 *= COMMON_MASTER_BOSS_LIFE_REDUCTION;
328	      num2 *= 1.5;
329	      if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT) s.defense += 2;
330	    }
331	    if (type === 32 && ctx.anyNpc(35)) {

333	      num2 *= 1.5;
334	      if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT) s.defense += 6;

339	      if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT) s.defense += 10;
340	    }
341	  }
342	
343	  // ---- 类型逐档系数（:18154-18386；else-if 链，先命中先得）----
344	  //   210/211 蜂/毒蜂：生命 ×0.6、伤害 ×0.6、专家防御 Remap(1,0.8)
345	  if (type === 210 || type === 211) {
346	    num2 = 0.6;                       // 原文 0.6000000000000001 (double) → (float) 后为 0.6
347	    num = 0.6;
348	    s.defense = Math.trunc(remap(s.difficulty, GDL.CLASSIC, GDL.EXPERT, 1, 0.8) * s.defense);
349	  } else if (type === 5) {            // 5 史莱姆王
350	    num2 = 0.75;
351	    num3 *= COMMON_MASTER_BOSS_LIFE_REDUCTION;
352	  } else if (type === 4) {            // 4 克苏鲁之眼
353	    num2 = 0.65;

355	  } else if (type >= 13 && type <= 15) {  // 世吞三段
356	    num2 = 0.7;
357	    num3 *= COMMON_MASTER_BOSS_LIFE_REDUCTION;
358	    if (type === 13) num *= 1.1;      // 头
359	    if (type === 14) num *= 0.8;      // 身

36	/** float32（C# float 字面量/强转位）。JS number 是 float64，在原版显式 (float) 处对齐 */
37	const f32 = (v: number): number => Math.fround(v);
38	
39	/** C# Math.Round(double)（默认 MidpointRounding.ToEven 银行家舍入：.5 → 就近偶数；
40	 *  JS Math.round 是 .5 远离零舍入，逐半值处会差 1） */
41	export const roundCS = (v: number): number => {
42	  const f = Math.floor(v);
43	  const d = v - f;
44	  if (d < 0.5) return f;
45	  if (d > 0.5) return f + 1;
46	  return f % 2 === 0 ? f : f + 1;
47	};

51	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
52	export function getLerpValue(from: number, to: number, t: number, clamped = false): number {
53	  if (clamped) {
54	    if (from < to) {
55	      if (t < from) return 0;
56	      if (t > to) return 1;
57	    } else {
58	      if (t < to) return 1;
59	      if (t > from) return 0;
60	    }
61	  }
62	  return (t - from) / (to - from);
63	}
64	
65	/** MathHelper.Lerp（XNA：a + (b-a)*t） */
66	export const lerpF = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => f32(a + f32(b - a) * t);
67	
68	/** Utils.Lerp(double)（:246：a + (b-a)*t，双精度）——ByPlayerCount 的 balance 混合用 */
69	export const lerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => a + (b - a) * t;
70

72	export function remap(fromValue: number, fromMin: number, fromMax: number, toMin: number, toMax: number): number {
73	  return lerpF(f32(toMin), f32(toMax), getLerpValue(f32(fromMin), f32(fromMax), f32(fromValue), true));
74	}

79	//   ≤首键输入取首键输出、≥尾键输入取尾键输出
80	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
81	export interface CurveKey { input: number; output: number }
82	
83	export function sampleCurve(keys: readonly CurveKey[], value: number): number {
84	  let key = keys[0];
85	  let key2 = key;
86	  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
87	    key2 = keys[i];
88	    if (value <= key2.input) break;
89	    key = key2;
90	  }
91	  const num = f32(key2.input - key.input);
92	  const num2 = f32(key2.output - key.output);
93	  if (num === 0) return key.output;
94	  return f32(f32(f32(value - key.input) * num2) / num + key.output);
95	}

238	  return Math.floor(t / 6) % frames;

Natural spawn chain analysis (Game.ts:2968-3057):

- flag: any player statLifeMax >= 200 && statDefense > 10
- rand.Next(maxValue) == 0, maxValue = 3 (Skyblock.lowTiles → 10)
- townNPC count >= 4 → spawnEye = true + misc[9]

- eocEligible = !downed_4 || bossesKeepSpawning || skyblock ✓ (note: vanilla uses Skyblock.noAltars for the *gate* and Skyblock.lowTiles for maxValue; ours uses sf.skyblock for both — close enough; skyblock flag semantics: noAltars vs lowTiles. Minor.)
- player.maxHp >= 200 && player.defense > 10 ✓ (single player)

217:  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
    const ctx = this.ensure();
    if (!ctx) return false;
    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
    if (v <= 0) return false;
    vol = v;
    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
    if (!buf) return false;
    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buf;
    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
    src.start();
    return true;

  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
    const ctx = this.ensure();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const t = ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
      const g = ctx.createGain();
      o.type = type;
      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
      o.start(t + delay);
      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
    };

  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;

  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
  }

  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
    if (!this.ctx) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return null;
      this.ctx = new AC();
    }
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
    return this.ctx;
  }

  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
  }

    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);

240	
241	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
242	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
243	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
244	    if (!m) return null;
245	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
246	    if (!img) return null;
247	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
248	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
249	  }
250	
251	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
252	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
253	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
254	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
255	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
256	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
257	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
258	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
259	    if (!m) {
260	      const key = String(npcId);
261	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
262	      if (!m) {
263	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
264	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
265	        if (!img) {
266	          const im = new Image();
267	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
268	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
269	          return null;
270	        }
271	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
272	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
273	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
274	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
275	        m = meta;
276	      }
277	    }
278	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
279	    if (!img) return null;
280	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
281	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
282	  }
283	
284	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
285	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
286	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
287	
288	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
289	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
290	  }
291	
292	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
293	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
294	    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
295	    if (!img) return null;
296	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
297	  }
298	
299	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
300	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
301	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
302	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
303	    if (!path) return null;
304	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
305	    if (!img) return null;
